# Skills · A · part 17

870 entries, ask to attacking-hardware-interfaces. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ask**: Answer a question about how the codebase works - research, not a fix. Load CANONIFY.md, route the question to the canons for its area, read them (and the code they cite) and produce a cited, developer-grade answer plus a short plain-language version you can hand to a client or stakeholder. No plan, no code, no diff. Run when the user types /canonify:ask, or says "a client is asking how X works", "does the system do Y", "what happens when Z", "how do we handle X", "explain how the W feature works", "I need to research a question about the project". ([ewebdzine/canonify](https://github.com/ewebdzine/canonify/tree/HEAD/skills/ask) · ★23)
- **ask**: Answer grounded questions about the codebase (onboarding / Q&A) using the reviewer RAG + code graph. Use when the user asks how the code works, where something lives, or to explain a subsystem ("where is auth", "how does X work", "explain the indexing flow", "как устроено…", "где у нас…", "объясни код"). Requires a built base index + graph (reviewer MCP server). Not for reviewing PRs. ([mimfort/rag_for_git](https://github.com/mimfort/rag_for_git/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/ask) · ★21)
- **ask**: Ask/decision tickets ("what needs me") — the Catalyst-wide SOP for raising a human decision as a Linear ticket, replying in-thread as the app actor, and closing it when the answer lands. Use whenever an agent needs a human to decide or click something, or for any ticket labelled `catalyst-ask` / `ask/decision`. ([coalesce-labs/catalyst](https://github.com/coalesce-labs/catalyst/tree/HEAD/plugins/dev/skills/ask) · ★20)
- **ask**: Single-model consultation using consultant agent. Defaults to gpt-5.2-pro. ([doodledood/claude-code-plugins](https://github.com/doodledood/claude-code-plugins/tree/HEAD/claude-plugins/consultant/skills/ask) · ★20)

- **ask-gate**: The Ask-Gate Protocol (AGP). Before surfacing an AskUserQuestion, triage whether the question is genuinely the handler's call (anti-nag); ask routine questions directly after a quality checklist, but escalate high-stakes / branching / irreversible ones through /insight-critique then /insight-assess first. Trigger via /ask-gate, or automatically before any handler-facing question. Not /caller-options (routes a skill's venue/mode) or /next-skills (recommends successors) — AGP governs the question itself. ([allemaar/open-skills](https://github.com/allemaar/open-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ask-gate) · ★14)

- **ask-questions-if-underspecified**: Use this skill when scope ambiguity blocks progress in any SOP step (PRD, TDD, design, task decomposition). Produces a tight set of clarifying questions (max 5) that, once answered, unblock the next step. Avoids the failure mode where Agents proceed on assumption and produce wrong work. Chinese trigger examples: "需求不清", "需要澄清", "提问澄清", "范围不明", "信息不足要澄清", "ask the user". Do NOT use for exploration (use research), do NOT use to delay obvious work. Success = ≤ 5 questions, each decision-shaping, each tied to a specific PRD section. ([charliehzm/medharness](https://github.com/charliehzm/medharness/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ask-questions-if-underspecified) · ★86)
- **ask-questions-if-underspecified**: Clarify requirements before implementing. Use when serious doubts araise. ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/ask-questions-if-underspecified) · ★64 · 2 copies)
- **ask-questions-if-underspecified**: Ask the minimum clarifying questions before implementation when requirements are ambiguous or missing crucial details ([oimiragieo/agent-studio](https://github.com/oimiragieo/agent-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ask-questions-if-underspecified) · ★41)
- **ask-questions-if-underspecified**: ALWAYS use this when the request matches ASK Questions IF Underspecified: Clarify requirements before implementing. ([Anhvu1107/all-agent-skill](https://github.com/Anhvu1107/all-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/ask-questions-if-underspecified) · ★25)
- **ask-questions-if-underspecified**: Pauses to ask focused clarifying questions when a request lacks enough information to execute correctly. Use when the task is vague, has multiple plausible interpretations, is missing acceptance criteria, or when wrong assumptions would produce significant wasted work.
